40:T418,<p>It seems Asura has its own comment section now...</p><p></p><p>Anyway, short review: This story is okay, in the beginning to when he confronts his father the story is good with a proper goal, but after that, it goes kinda downhill and becomes repetitive and even boring to read in my opinion. There&#039;s also this one controversy where the mc r*pes a woman, which many people are upset about</p><p></p><p>But it&#039;s still a solid read with amazing art, so 7.8/10<br><br>Edit: Since some people in the replies have pointed out that the mc only did it with her because he wanted to save her, I wanted to edit my thoughts again: I am aware that he did it with her ONLY because he wanted to save her and her father agreed, but the problem is the authors choice of writing; there are multiple ways the author could have handled it, and there are other ways to transfer yin energy (I think he wanted to do that if i remember correctly), so there was no reason for the r*pe scene at all (yes it&#039;s r*pe since she didn&#039;t agree to that)</p>41:T430,<p>Pros</p><p>This manhwa have very good art. In fact the arts is its strongest quality. Tha action and fight scenses are crisp and detailed . Characters and creature design are unique (though they sometimes suffer from same face syndrome) and while it&#039;s not a gorefest artist doesn&#039;t shy away from showing the brutality of of the actions committed . That said while the visuals is good is never great or awesomely memorable.</p><p>8/10</p><p></p><p>Cons</p><p>The writing is where this manhwa really dropped the ball. The MC/Protagonist is a bland boring ruthless Gary Stu, the plot is repetitive, uninspired and tepid and all of the characters are two dimensional at best . That is before some of the stupid problematic writing choice such as the infamous &quot;rape&quot; plot point.</p><p>NOTE: I&#039;m not saying thing like rape shouldn&#039;t/couldn&#039;t be used in a s tory . I would not have a problem if it was written well and be given the proper weight . ESPECIALLY WHEN YOU MAKE THE MC/PROTAGANIST AS THE RAPIST.</p><p><br>6.5/10</p><p></p><p></p>42:T415,<p><strong><em>Review time -&gt;</em></strong></p><p></p><p>&quot;This manhua is definitely one of the best in the Morim-type category. The MC is quite decent, and if you&#039;ve read Myst Mythe Mayem, you&#039;ll feel it even more. The story is connected to it, as this one features the second Heavenly Demon, while Myst Mythe Mayem focuses on the first Heavenly Demon.</p><p>The plot starts with a boy whose mother is sick. After her death, the MC begins his character development. Eventually, he takes on the role of his father, though it&#039;s not an easy task. The story writing is solid, and the overall progression is engaging.</p><p>You don’t have to worry about multiple waifus here, as the MC is quite lenient about it. This is mainly because of his half-brother, who is trying to wage war for the throne. The MC isn&#039;t particularly interested in Heaven, but you can expect at least two waifus to be involved.</p><p>Overall, I’d rate it 9 out of 10, personally. It’s definitely a great read and highly recommended.&quot;</p>43:T40e,<p>Murirm, highly advanced tech, and lots and lots of severed arms. There&#039;s a lot to like.  A good amount of side characters are fleshed out.  Plenty to like and plenty to dislike.  Same issues with all manwhas with op MC.</p><p></p><p>Once the mc becomes too powerful everyone else is just there as crowd noise.  This Series is a sequel to Might Myst and Mayhem as well as Absolute Sword Sense.  It&#039;s really cool because you&#039;ll kind of learn some lore in different perspectives.  What&#039;s not cool is the translations are all different so certain legendary items are called something else or techniques.</p><p></p><p>Out of the three I like this one the most its had quite a few downs once you go past 100 chapters.  For instance a particular problem that has to be solved by a wtf answer.  Author could have gone a dozen other routes but specifically chose that one answer.</p><p></p><p>The pacing is the fastest out of the three and the fighting artwork is arguably the best too.  I&#039;d say an 8-8.5 out of 10.</p>44:T419,<p>solid 10/10 until he becomes the Heavenly D*mon, but then the story starts going downhill. start becoming too powerful too fast, and he doesn&#039;t even
